### فایل `eia-weekly-stocks/scripts/analyze_wpsr.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Analyze EIA WPSR data: changes, 5-year comparison, signals and quality gates.

Usage:
  python analyze_wpsr.py --data data/wpsr_long.csv --out data/analysis.json
         [--consensus consensus.json] [--previous data/analysis_prev.json] [--asof YYYY-MM-DD]
"""
import argparse, datetime as dt, json, math
import pandas as pd

NAMES_FA = {
    "WCESTUS1": "موجودی تجاری نفت خام",
    "W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL": "موجودی کاشینگ",
    "WCSSTUS1": "ذخایر استراتژیک (SPR)",
    "WGTSTUS1": "موجودی بنزین",
    "WDISTUS1": "موجودی تقطیری",
    "WKJSTUS1": "موجودی سوخت جت",
    "WPULEUS3": "نرخ بهره‌برداری پالایشگاه‌ها",
    "WCRRIUS2": "خوراک نفت خام پالایشگاه‌ها",
    "WCRFPUS2": "تولید نفت خام",
    "WCEIMUS2": "واردات نفت خام",
    "WCREXUS2": "صادرات نفت خام",
    "WRPUPUS2": "مصرف ضمنی کل",
    "WGFUPUS2": "مصرف ضمنی بنزین",
    "WDIUPUS2": "مصرف ضمنی تقطیری",
    "WKJUPUS2": "مصرف ضمنی سوخت جت",
}
CORE = ["WCESTUS1", "W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL", "WCSSTUS1", "WGTSTUS1", "WDISTUS1", "WPULEUS3",
        "WCRRIUS2", "WCRFPUS2", "WRPUPUS2", "WGFUPUS2", "WDIUPUS2"]
STOCKS = {"WCESTUS1", "W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL", "WCSSTUS1", "WGTSTUS1", "WDISTUS1", "WKJSTUS1"}
# signal topic: (series, threshold vs 5y change in mb, threshold vs consensus in mb)
SIGNAL_RULES = {"crude": ("WCESTUS1", 1.5, 2.0), "cushing": ("W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL", 0.5, 0.75),
                "gasoline": ("WGTSTUS1", 1.0, 1.5), "distillate": ("WDISTUS1", 1.0, 1.5)}
DIR_FA = {"supportive": "حمایتی برای قیمت",
          "bearish": "فشار نزولی", "neutral": "خنثی"}


def r(x, n=2):
    return None if x is None or (isinstance(x, float) and math.isnan(x)) else round(float(x), n)


def scale(sid, v):
    return v / 1000.0 if sid in STOCKS else v  # kbbl -> mb


def iso_wk(ts):
    y, w, _ = ts.isocalendar()
    return int(y), min(int(w), 52)


def analyze_series(s, sid):
    s = s.dropna().sort_index()
    if len(s) < 2:
        return None
    t = s.index[-1]
    latest, prev = s.iloc[-1], s.iloc[-2]
    ch = s.diff()
    year_ago = s[(s.index >= t - pd.Timedelta(days=367)) & (s.index <= t - pd.Timedelta(days=361))]
    y, w = iso_wk(t)
    levels, changes = [], []
    for k in range(1, 6):
        m = [i for i in s.index if iso_wk(i) == (y - k, w)]
        if m:
            levels.append(s[m[0]]); changes.append(ch[m[0]])
    hist = ch.iloc[-157:-1].dropna()
    z = (ch.iloc[-1] - hist.mean()) / hist.std() if len(hist) > 20 and hist.std() > 0 else None
    out = {
        "name_fa": NAMES_FA.get(sid, sid),
        "unit": "mb" if sid in STOCKS else ("pct" if sid == "WPULEUS3" else "kbd"),
        "period": t.date().isoformat(),
        "latest": r(scale(sid, latest)),
        "wow": r(scale(sid, latest - prev)),
        "yoy": r(scale(sid, latest - year_ago.iloc[-1])) if len(year_ago) else None,
        "avg5y_level": r(scale(sid, sum(levels) / len(levels))) if levels else None,
        "avg5y_change": r(scale(sid, sum(changes) / len(changes))) if changes else None,
        "min5y": r(scale(sid, min(levels))) if levels else None,
        "max5y": r(scale(sid, max(levels))) if levels else None,
        "z_change": r(z),
        "years_in_5y": len(levels),
        "history_tail": {i.date().isoformat(): float(v) for i, v in s.iloc[-8:].items()},
    }
    if out["avg5y_level"] not in (None, 0):
        out["dev_vs_5y"] = r(out["latest"] - out["avg5y_level"])
        out["dev_vs_5y_pct"] = r(100 * out["dev_vs_5y"] / out["avg5y_level"], 1)
    if out["avg5y_change"] is not None:
        out["rel_vs_5y_change"] = r(out["wow"] - out["avg5y_change"])
    return out


def avg4(df, sid, end, offset_days=0):
    if sid not in df:
        return None
    s = df[sid].dropna()
    s = s[s.index <= end - pd.Timedelta(days=offset_days)]
    return float(s.iloc[-4:].mean()) if len(s) >= 4 else None


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--data", required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--consensus"); ap.add_argument("--previous"); ap.add_argument("--asof")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    asof = dt.date.fromisoformat(a.asof) if a.asof else dt.date.today()
    long = pd.read_csv(a.data, parse_dates=["period"])
    df = long.pivot_table(index="period", columns="series_id", values="value", aggfunc="last").sort_index()
    issues, block = [], False

    missing_core = [c for c in CORE if c not in df or df[c].dropna().empty]
    if missing_core:
        block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: missing core series {missing_core}")
    optional = [c for c in NAMES_FA if c not in CORE]
    miss_opt = [c for c in optional if c not in df or df[c].dropna().empty]
    if miss_opt:
        issues.append(f"WARN: missing optional series {miss_opt}")

    series = {sid: res for sid in df.columns if (res := analyze_series(df[sid], sid))}
    weeks = {series[c]["period"] for c in CORE if c in series}
    report_week = max(weeks) if weeks else None
    if len(weeks) > 1:
        block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: core series end on different weeks {sorted(weeks)}")
    if report_week and (asof - dt.date.fromisoformat(report_week)).days > 13:
        block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: latest week {report_week} is stale as of {asof}")
    for sid, v in series.items():
        if sid in STOCKS and v["latest"] is not None and v["latest"] < 0:
            block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: negative stock value in {sid}")
        if v["z_change"] is not None and abs(v["z_change"]) > 4:
            issues.append(f"WARN: unusual weekly change in {sid} (z={v['z_change']})")

    if a.previous:
        prev = json.load(open(a.previous, encoding="utf-8"))
        for sid, v in series.items():
            old = prev.get("series", {}).get(sid, {}).get("history_tail", {})
            for p, val in old.items():
                new = v["history_tail"].get(p)
                if new is not None and abs(new - val) > 1e-9:
                    issues.append(f"WARN: revision in {sid} for {p}: {val} -> {new}")

    cons = json.load(open(a.consensus, encoding="utf-8")) if a.consensus else None
    if cons:
        exp = cons.get("expected_change", {})
        for sid, e in exp.items():
            if sid in series:
                series[sid]["expected"] = e
                series[sid]["surprise"] = r(series[sid]["wow"] - e)
        if len(exp) < 3:
            issues.append("WARN: consensus covers fewer than 3 series")

    end = pd.Timestamp(report_week) if report_week else df.index.max()
    derived = {}
    for key, sid in [("implied_demand_total", "WRPUPUS2"), ("implied_demand_gasoline", "WGFUPUS2"),
                     ("implied_demand_distillate", "WDIUPUS2")]:
        now, ago = avg4(df, sid, end), avg4(df, sid, end, 364)
        derived[key] = {"avg4w_kbd": r(now, 0), "avg4w_year_ago_kbd": r(ago, 0),
                        "yoy_pct": r(100 * (now - ago) / ago, 1) if now and ago else None}
    for key, stock, flow in [("crude_days_supply", "WCESTUS1", "WCRRIUS2"),
                             ("gasoline_days_supply", "WGTSTUS1", "WGFUPUS2"),
                             ("distillate_days_supply", "WDISTUS1", "WDIUPUS2")]:
        f = avg4(df, flow, end)
        derived[key] = r(df[stock].dropna().iloc[-1] / f, 1) if f and stock in df else None
    big3 = [series[s]["wow"] for s in ("WCESTUS1", "WGTSTUS1", "WDISTUS1") if s in series]
    derived["commercial_big3_change_mb"] = r(sum(big3)) if len(big3) == 3 else None

    signals = []
    for topic, (sid, th5, thc) in SIGNAL_RULES.items():
        v = series.get(sid)
        if not v:
            continue
        basis, val, th = ("consensus", v.get("surprise"), thc) if v.get("surprise") is not None \
            else ("5y", v.get("rel_vs_5y_change"), th5)
        if val is None:
            continue
        direction = "supportive" if val < -th else ("bearish" if val > th else "neutral")
        signals.append({"topic": topic, "series": sid, "basis": basis, "value_mb": val,
                        "direction": direction, "direction_fa": DIR_FA[direction],
                        "strength": "strong" if abs(val) >= 2 * th else ("moderate" if abs(val) > th else "weak"),
                        "score": r(abs(val) / th)})
    d = derived["implied_demand_total"]["yoy_pct"]
    if d is not None:
        direction = "supportive" if d > 2 else ("bearish" if d < -2 else "neutral")
        signals.append({"topic": "demand", "series": "WRPUPUS2", "basis": "yoy_4w", "value_pct": d,
                        "direction": direction, "direction_fa": DIR_FA[direction],
                        "strength": "moderate" if abs(d) > 2 else "weak", "score": r(abs(d) / 2)})
    signals.sort(key=lambda s: s["score"] or 0, reverse=True)

    status = "BLOCK" if block else ("WARN" if issues else "OK")
    result = {"report_week": report_week, "generated_at": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
              "asof": asof.isoformat(), "series": series, "derived": derived, "signals": signals,
              "quality": {"status": status, "issues": issues},
              "sources": ["EIA Weekly Petroleum Status Report via EIA API v2"] + ([cons.get("source", "consensus")] if cons else [])}
    json.dump(result, open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print(f"report_week={report_week} status={status} issues={len(issues)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `eia-weekly-stocks/scripts/fetch_wpsr.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fetch EIA Weekly Petroleum Status Report series via EIA API v2.

Usage:
  python fetch_wpsr.py --out data/wpsr_long.csv --raw-dir data/raw --years 7
  python fetch_wpsr.py --out data/wpsr_long.csv --wait-for-new --timeout-min 90

Reads the API key from the EIA_API_KEY environment variable. Never prints it.
"""
import argparse, csv, datetime as dt, hashlib, json, os, sys, time
import requests

API = "https://api.eia.gov/v2/seriesid/PET.{sid}.W"

# id: (unit, role)  -- keep in sync with references/series.md
SERIES = {
    "WCESTUS1": ("kbbl", "core"),
    "W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL": ("kbbl", "core"),
    "WCSSTUS1": ("kbbl", "core"),
    "WGTSTUS1": ("kbbl", "core"),
    "WDISTUS1": ("kbbl", "core"),
    "WKJSTUS1": ("kbbl", "optional"),
    "WPULEUS3": ("pct", "core"),
    "WCRRIUS2": ("kbd", "core"),
    "WCRFPUS2": ("kbd", "core"),
    "WCEIMUS2": ("kbd", "optional"),
    "WCREXUS2": ("kbd", "optional"),
    "WRPUPUS2": ("kbd", "core"),
    "WGFUPUS2": ("kbd", "core"),
    "WDIUPUS2": ("kbd", "core"),
    "WKJUPUS2": ("kbd", "optional"),
}


def fetch_series(sid, key, start, retries=4):
    params = {"api_key": key, "start": start, "length": 5000}
    delay = 2
    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            r = requests.get(API.format(sid=sid), params=params, timeout=30)
            if r.status_code == 200:
                return r.content
            if r.status_code in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                time.sleep(delay); delay *= 2; continue
            print(f"[error] {sid}: HTTP {r.status_code}", file=sys.stderr)
            return None
        except requests.RequestException as exc:
            print(f"[retry] {sid}: {type(exc).__name__}", file=sys.stderr)
            time.sleep(delay); delay *= 2
    return None


def parse(content):
    payload = json.loads(content)
    rows = payload.get("response", {}).get("data", [])
    out = []
    for row in rows:
        val = row.get("value")
        if val in (None, ""):
            continue
        out.append((row["period"], float(val)))
    return sorted(out)


def latest_period_in(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return None
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        periods = [r["period"] for r in csv.DictReader(fh) if r["series_id"] == "WCESTUS1"]
    return max(periods) if periods else None


def run_once(args, key):
    start = (dt.date.today() - dt.timedelta(days=365 * args.years + 14)).isoformat()
    fetched_at = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    os.makedirs(args.raw_dir, exist_ok=True)
    rows, missing, manifest = [], [], []
    for sid, (unit, role) in SERIES.items():
        content = fetch_series(sid, key, start)
        if content is None:
            missing.append((sid, role)); continue
        digest = hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()
        stamp = fetched_at.replace(":", "")
        raw_path = os.path.join(args.raw_dir, f"{sid}_{stamp}.json")
        with open(raw_path, "wb") as fh:
            fh.write(content)
        data = parse(content)
        if not data:
            missing.append((sid, role)); continue
        manifest.append({"series_id": sid, "sha256": digest, "raw": raw_path, "rows": len(data)})
        rows += [(sid, p, v, unit, fetched_at) for p, v in data]
        time.sleep(0.3)  # be polite to the API
    return rows, missing, manifest


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--raw-dir", default="data/raw")
    ap.add_argument("--years", type=int, default=7)
    ap.add_argument("--wait-for-new", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--timeout-min", type=int, default=90)
    ap.add_argument("--poll-sec", type=int, default=120)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    key = os.environ.get("EIA_API_KEY")
    if not key:
        sys.exit("EIA_API_KEY is not set")

    previous = latest_period_in(args.out)
    deadline = time.time() + args.timeout_min * 60
    while True:
        rows, missing, manifest = run_once(args, key)
        newest = max((p for s, p, *_ in rows if s == "WCESTUS1"), default=None)
        if not args.wait_for_new or (newest and (previous is None or newest > previous)):
            break
        if time.time() > deadline:
            print(f"[stale] no new week after {args.timeout_min} min; latest={newest}", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(3)
        time.sleep(args.poll_sec)

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.out) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    with open(args.out, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        w = csv.writer(fh)
        w.writerow(["series_id", "period", "value", "unit", "fetched_at"])
        w.writerows(rows)
    with open(os.path.join(args.raw_dir, "manifest.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump({"fetched": manifest, "missing": missing}, fh, indent=2)

    print(f"rows={len(rows)} latest_week={newest} missing={[s for s, _ in missing]}")
    if any(role == "core" for _, role in missing):
        sys.exit(2)  # core series missing: the analysis step will BLOCK


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `eia-weekly-stocks/scripts/render_brief_fa.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Render the Persian executive brief from analysis.json.

Usage: python render_brief_fa.py --analysis data/analysis.json --out data/brief_fa.md
"""
import argparse, json, sys

FA = str.maketrans("0123456789.-+", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹٫−+")
UNIT_FA = {"mb": "میلیون بشکه", "kbd": "هزار بشکه در روز", "pct": "درصد"}
ROWS = ["WCESTUS1", "W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL", "WGTSTUS1", "WDISTUS1", "WKJSTUS1", "WCSSTUS1",
        "WPULEUS3", "WCRRIUS2", "WCRFPUS2"]
TOPIC_FA = {"crude": "نفت خام", "cushing": "کاشینگ", "gasoline": "بنزین",
            "distillate": "تقطیری", "demand": "تقاضای ضمنی"}
UP, DOWN = "افزایش", "کاهش"


def fa(x, signed=False, nd=1):
    if x is None:
        return "—"
    s = f"{x:+.{nd}f}" if signed else f"{x:,.{nd}f}".replace(",", "٬")
    return s.translate(FA)


def headline(a):
    sig = next((s for s in a["signals"] if s["topic"] != "demand"), None)
    if not sig:
        return "داده این هفته سیگنال مشخصی ندارد."
    v = a["series"][sig["series"]]
    move = DOWN if v["wow"] < 0 else UP
    text = f"{v['name_fa']} آمریکا {fa(abs(v['wow']))} میلیون بشکه {move} یافت"
    if sig["basis"] == "consensus":
        diff = "کمتر" if v["surprise"] < 0 else "بیشتر"
        text += f"، {fa(abs(v['surprise']))} میلیون بشکه {diff} از انتظار"
    elif v.get("avg5y_change") is not None:
        text += f" (میانگین پنج‌ساله همین هفته: {fa(v['avg5y_change'], True)})"
    return text + f"؛ ارزیابی: {sig['direction_fa']}."


def table(a):
    head = "| شاخص | سطح | تغییر هفتگی | میانگین تغییر ۵ ساله | فاصله از میانگین ۵ ساله | واحد |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|"
    lines = [head]
    for sid in ROWS:
        v = a["series"].get(sid)
        if not v:
            continue
        dev = f"{fa(v.get('dev_vs_5y'), True)} ({fa(v.get('dev_vs_5y_pct'), True)}٪)" if v.get("dev_vs_5y") is not None else "—"
        lines.append(f"| {v['name_fa']} | {fa(v['latest'])} | {fa(v['wow'], True)} | {fa(v.get('avg5y_change'), True)} | {dev} | {UNIT_FA[v['unit']]} |")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def signals(a):
    out = []
    for s in a["signals"]:
        if s["topic"] == "demand":
            out.append(f"- **{TOPIC_FA['demand']}** (میانگین ۴ هفته نسبت به پارسال): {fa(s['value_pct'], True)}٪ → {s['direction_fa']}")
        else:
            basis = "نسبت به انتظار" if s["basis"] == "consensus" else "نسبت به الگوی فصلی ۵ ساله"
            out.append(f"- **{TOPIC_FA[s['topic']]}** ({basis}): {fa(s['value_mb'], True)} میلیون بشکه → {s['direction_fa']}")
    return "\n".join(out) or "-"


def details(a):
    d = a["derived"]
    rows = []
    for key, label in [("implied_demand_total", "مصرف ضمنی کل"), ("implied_demand_gasoline", "مصرف ضمنی بنزین"), ("implied_demand_distillate", "مصرف ضمنی تقطیری")]:
        x = d.get(key) or {}
        rows.append(f"- {label}: میانگین ۴ هفته {fa(x.get('avg4w_kbd'), nd=0)} هزار بشکه در روز (پارسال {fa(x.get('avg4w_year_ago_kbd'), nd=0)}، تغییر {fa(x.get('yoy_pct'), True)}٪)")
    for key, label in [("crude_days_supply", "نفت خام"), ("gasoline_days_supply", "بنزین"), ("distillate_days_supply", "تقطیری")]:
        rows.append(f"- روزهای پوشش {label}: {fa(d.get(key))} روز")
    rows.append(f"- مجموع تغییر نفت خام، بنزین و تقطیری (تجاری): {fa(d.get('commercial_big3_change_mb'), True)} میلیون بشکه")
    spr = a["series"].get("WCSSTUS1")
    if spr:
        rows.append(f"- SPR (جدا از موجودی تجاری، تصمیم سیاستی): {fa(spr['latest'])} میلیون بشکه، تغییر {fa(spr['wow'], True)}")
    return "\n".join(rows)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--analysis", required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--template", default=None)
    a_ = ap.parse_args()
    a = json.load(open(a_.analysis, encoding="utf-8"))
    q = a["quality"]
    if q["status"] == "BLOCK":
        open(a_.out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("# بریف منتشر نشد\n\n" + "\n".join(f"- {i}" for i in q["issues"]))
        print("BLOCK: brief not published"); sys.exit(1)
    qtext = "بدون هشدار" if q["status"] == "OK" else "\n".join(f"- ⚠ {i}" for i in q["issues"])
    week = a["report_week"].translate(str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"))
    body = (f"# بریف هفتگی موجودی نفت آمریکا، هفته منتهی به {week}\n\n"
            f"> **در ۵ ثانیه:** {headline(a)}\n\n"
            f"## در ۳۰ ثانیه: اعداد کلیدی\n{table(a)}\n\n"
            f"## سیگنال‌ها\n{signals(a)}\n\n"
            f"## در ۵ دقیقه: جزئیات\n{details(a)}\n\n"
            f"## کیفیت داده\n{qtext}\n\n---\n"
            f"منبع: EIA Weekly Petroleum Status Report · تولید خودکار در {a['generated_at']}\n")
    open(a_.out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(body)
    print(f"brief written: {a_.out} ({q['status']})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `eia-weekly-stocks/tests/test_synthetic.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline test on SYNTHETIC data (not real EIA numbers). Run: python tests/test_synthetic.py"""
import json, math, os, random, subprocess, sys, tempfile
import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SCRIPTS = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "scripts")
BASE = {"WCESTUS1": (430000, 15000), "W_EPC0_SAX_YCUOK_MBBL": (25000, 3000), "WCSSTUS1": (400000, 0),
        "WGTSTUS1": (225000, 12000), "WDISTUS1": (120000, 10000), "WKJSTUS1": (43000, 2000),
        "WPULEUS3": (90, 3), "WCRRIUS2": (16200, 600), "WCRFPUS2": (13300, 0), "WCEIMUS2": (6300, 0),
        "WCREXUS2": (4000, 0), "WRPUPUS2": (20300, 600), "WGFUPUS2": (8900, 500), "WDIUPUS2": (3800, 200),
        "WKJUPUS2": (1700, 100)}


def make_csv(path, end="2026-10-16", years=7, seed=7):
    rnd = random.Random(seed)
    dates = pd.date_range(end=end, periods=52 * years + 2, freq="W-FRI")
    rows = []
    for sid, (base, amp) in BASE.items():
        for d in dates:
            season = amp * math.sin(2 * math.pi * d.dayofyear / 365.25)
            noise = rnd.gauss(0, max(base * 0.004, 0.2))
            rows.append((sid, d.date().isoformat(), round(base + season + noise, 1), "x", "synthetic"))
    pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["series_id", "period", "value", "unit", "fetched_at"]).to_csv(path, index=False)


def main():
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    csv, out, brief = (os.path.join(tmp, n) for n in ("d.csv", "a.json", "b.md"))
    make_csv(csv)
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(SCRIPTS, "analyze_wpsr.py"), "--data", csv, "--out", out,
                    "--asof", "2026-10-21"], check=True)
    a = json.load(open(out, encoding="utf-8"))
    assert a["report_week"] == "2026-10-16", a["report_week"]
    assert a["quality"]["status"] in ("OK", "WARN"), a["quality"]
    assert a["series"]["WCESTUS1"]["years_in_5y"] == 5
    assert a["derived"]["crude_days_supply"] is not None
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(SCRIPTS, "render_brief_fa.py"), "--analysis", out, "--out", brief], check=True)
    text = open(brief, encoding="utf-8").read()
    assert "۲۰۲۶" in text  # Persian-digit year in the title
    # stale data must BLOCK
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(SCRIPTS, "analyze_wpsr.py"), "--data", csv, "--out", out,
                        "--asof", "2026-11-20"], check=True)
    assert json.load(open(out, encoding="utf-8"))["quality"]["status"] == "BLOCK"
    print("ALL TESTS PASSED")
    print(text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ۷-۲. oil-shock-decomposition

فایل‌ها: `oil-shock-decomposition/SKILL.md`، `oil-shock-decomposition/README_fa.md`، `oil-shock-decomposition/references/data_sources.md`، `oil-shock-decomposition/references/interpretation.md`، `oil-shock-decomposition/references/methods.md`، `oil-shock-decomposition/scripts/decompose.py`، `oil-shock-decomposition/scripts/fetch_inputs.py`، `oil-shock-decomposition/tests/test_synthetic.py`

### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/SKILL.md`

---
name: oil-shock-decomposition
description: Explain why the real price of crude oil moved by decomposing it into oil supply shocks, global (economic) demand shocks and oil-specific (precautionary/inventory) demand shocks. Estimates the Kilian (2009) structural VAR in-house, then triangulates with the published Baumeister-Hamilton (2019) structural shocks and the Kanzig (2021) OPEC supply-news shocks, and writes a Persian executive brief that shows where the methods agree and disagree. Use for monthly market reviews, "why did oil rise/fall" questions, scenario framing and board briefings.
---

#### Oil Shock Decomposition

Answers the executive question "why did the oil price move?" with established, peer-reviewed
methods instead of narrative guesswork. Output is a contribution estimate per driver, the agreement
across identification schemes, and a Persian brief.

##### When to use
- Monthly market review after new production, activity and price data are available.
- A user asks what drove a price move over the last 1, 3 or 12 months.
- Before scenario analysis, to establish which driver currently dominates.

Do NOT use for: intraday or weekly moves (the models are monthly with a 2 to 3 month data lag;
use the eia-weekly-stocks and news skills for the latest weeks), price forecasting (use
forecast-benchmark), or single-event attribution without data.

##### Methods
1. **Kilian (2009) recursive SVAR, computed here** (`scripts/decompose.py`).
   Variables (monthly): percent change in world crude production, Kilian real economic activity
   index (IGREA), log real oil price. 24 lags by default, Cholesky ordering
   production > activity > price. Shocks: oil supply, aggregate demand, oil-specific demand.
   Produces impulse responses (optional bootstrap bands) and a historical decomposition.
2. **Baumeister-Hamilton (2019), ingested.** Bayesian SVAR with priors on supply and demand
   elasticities, including inventories. Load the structural shocks the authors publish
   (https://sites.google.com/site/cjsbaumeister/research) or run the MIT-licensed Python port
   https://github.com/nkoutoun/bh2019-oil-svar-python, then pass the shocks CSV with `--bh`.
3. **Kanzig (2021) OPEC supply-news shocks, ingested.** High-frequency surprise in oil futures
   around OPEC announcements, aggregated monthly. Data: https://github.com/dkaenzig/oilsupplynews
   (CC BY 4.0). Pass with `--kaenzig`.

The methods disagree in known ways (Kilian assumes supply does not respond within the month to
demand shocks; Baumeister-Hamilton estimate that elasticity). Showing them side by side is the point.

##### Requirements
- Python 3.10+, `numpy`, `pandas`; `requests` only for the optional fetcher.
- Optional keys for the fetcher: `EIA_API_KEY`, `FRED_API_KEY` (environment variables; never print).

##### Workflow
1. **Prepare inputs** (either):
   - `python scripts/fetch_inputs.py --out data/kilian_inputs.csv` (EIA + FRED), or
   - supply your own CSV with columns `month, world_crude_prod_kbd, igrea, oil_price_nominal, us_cpi`.
   See `references/data_sources.md` for sources and the series to verify.
2. **Decompose and triangulate**:
   `python scripts/decompose.py --data data/kilian_inputs.csv --out data/shocks.json --brief data/brief_fa.md [--bh bh_shocks.csv --bh-sign more_supply|price_raising] [--kaenzig kaenzig_monthly.csv] [--lags 24] [--bootstrap 300]`
3. **Gate**: if `quality.status` is `BLOCK`, report the reasons and stop.
4. **Review** with `references/interpretation.md`, section "Before you publish". Change wording only.
5. **Deliver** `shocks.json` and `brief_fa.md` to the destination the calling job names.

##### Interpretation rules (summary)
- Contributions are model estimates under a stated identification, not facts. Always name the method.
- Lead with the driver that explains the largest share of the 12-month change, then the last 3 months.
- If the methods disagree on the sign of the supply shock, say so explicitly; do not pick a winner.
- Report the data cut-off month; never present the latest model month as "this month".
- The residual "base" term (initial conditions and constant) is reported, not hidden.

##### Quality gates (enforced by decompose.py)
- `BLOCK`: missing months inside the sample; fewer than 120 usable observations; non-positive
  prices or CPI; VAR not stable (largest companion root >= 1).
- `WARN`: latest month older than 4 months at run time; largest root > 0.98; BH or Kanzig file
  does not cover the latest model month; fewer than 5 observations per equation parameter.

##### Output contract
`shocks.json`: `spec` (variables, lags, sample, identification), `latest_month`,
`decomposition` (for windows 1, 3, 12 months: price change in percent and the contribution of each
shock plus base), `irf` (horizons 0 to 18, optional bands), `structural_shocks_tail` (last 24 months),
`triangulation` (per month: Kilian, BH and Kanzig supply-shock signs and the agreement label),
`quality`, `sources`. `brief_fa.md`: 5-second headline, 30-second contribution table, 5-minute
detail with method comparison and caveats.

##### Files
- `scripts/fetch_inputs.py`: optional EIA and FRED fetcher (series IDs to verify).
- `scripts/decompose.py`: SVAR, impulse responses, bootstrap, historical decomposition, triangulation, Persian brief.
- `references/methods.md`: equations, identification assumptions, citations.
- `references/data_sources.md`: inputs, IDs, update cadence, licences.
- `references/interpretation.md`: reading the results, pitfalls, pre-publish checklist.
- `tests/test_synthetic.py`: offline test on simulated data with known shocks.
