### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/references/data_sources.md`

#### Data sources

| Column | Meaning | Source | ID / route | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| world_crude_prod_kbd | World crude oil incl. lease condensate production, kb/d | EIA International Energy Statistics API v2 | route international, productId 57, activityId 1, countryRegionId WORL, unit TBPD | verify IDs at first run |
| igrea | Kilian index of global real economic activity | Dallas Fed via FRED | FRED series IGREA | confirmed |
| oil_price_nominal | US refiner acquisition cost of imported crude, USD/bbl | EIA API v2 | seriesid PET.R1300____3.M | verify ID at first run |
| us_cpi | US CPI-U, all items, SA | BLS via FRED | FRED series CPIAUCSL | confirmed |

Alternative price: Brent spot monthly average (FRED MCOILBRENTEU) if the refiner acquisition cost
is unavailable; state the change in the brief because results shift slightly.

Publication lag: production and refiner cost data arrive with about 2 to 3 months delay. The model
therefore always ends 2 to 3 months before the run date.

Optional files:
- BH shocks (`--bh`): CSV with `month` and at least `oil_supply`; optional `economic_activity`,
  `consumption_demand`, `inventory_demand`. Download from the authors' page and convert, or export
  from the Python port. Sign convention in the BH files: positive oil supply shock means more supply;
  the script flips it so positive means price-raising (default `--bh-sign more_supply`; pass `--bh-sign price_raising` if your file already uses that convention). Confirm the convention in the file you download.
- Kanzig shocks (`--kaenzig`): CSV with `month` and `oil_supply_news` (positive means price-raising).

Licences: FRED and EIA data are public. Kanzig data CC BY 4.0 (cite). BH shocks: academic use with
citation; check terms before commercial redistribution.

### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/references/interpretation.md`

#### Interpretation

##### How to read the output
- **Window contributions** (1, 3, 12 months) answer "of the X percent change in the real oil price,
  how much came from each driver". They add up with the base term.
- **Oil-specific demand** captures precautionary buying, inventory demand and shifts in expectations
  (for example geopolitical risk). It is often the largest short-run driver.
- **Aggregate demand** reflects the global business cycle seen through shipping activity.
- **Supply** shocks are typically smaller in variance but matter around OPEC+ decisions,
  outages and sanctions.

##### Pitfalls
- Do not translate a contribution into a dollar amount with false precision; round to whole percent.
- A large base term means initial conditions dominate; say that the window is too short to judge.
- When Kilian and BH disagree on supply, the difference usually comes from the supply elasticity
  assumption. State it plainly: "the conclusion depends on how quickly producers react".
- Real price, not nominal: say "real price (inflation adjusted)" in the brief.

##### Language for the Persian brief
- Use «سهم تقریبی» (approximate contribution), never «علت قطعی».
- Name the method in every contribution line: Kilian (2009), Baumeister–Hamilton (2019), Kanzig (2021).
- State the data cut-off month in the headline.

##### Before you publish
- quality.status is not BLOCK; every WARN appears in the brief.
- The cut-off month in the brief equals latest_month.
- The three contributions plus base equal the window change (the script checks this; verify the line).
- Any method disagreement is visible in the brief.
- No number typed by hand.

### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/references/methods.md`

#### Methods

##### Kilian (2009) structural VAR
Reduced form: y_t = c + A_1 y_{t-1} + ... + A_p y_{t-p} + u_t, with y_t = (dprod_t, rea_t, rpo_t)
- dprod_t: 100 x log change of world crude production
- rea_t: Kilian real economic activity index (IGREA, already detrended)
- rpo_t: 100 x log of the real oil price (nominal price deflated by US CPI)

Identification: u_t = B0inv e_t with B0inv lower triangular (Cholesky of the residual covariance).
- e1 oil supply shock: the only shock allowed to move production within the month.
- e2 aggregate demand shock: moves activity and price within the month, not production.
- e3 oil-specific demand shock: moves only the price within the month (precautionary or
  inventory demand, expectations of future shortfalls).

Structural MA: Theta_i = Phi_i B0inv, where Phi_i are the reduced-form MA coefficients.
Historical decomposition of rpo at time t: sum over j of sum_{i=0}^{t-1} Theta_i[3,j] e_{j,t-i};
the remainder (actual minus the three contributions) is the base term.
Window contributions are differences of the decomposition between the window start and end, so
they add up to the actual change in 100 x log real price.

Bootstrap: recursive-design residual bootstrap of the VAR (default 300 draws) for 68 and 90 percent
impulse-response bands.

Citation: Kilian, L. (2009), "Not All Oil Price Shocks Are Alike", American Economic Review 99(3).
Known critique: assumes a vertical short-run supply curve; see Baumeister and Hamilton (2019).

##### Baumeister-Hamilton (2019)
Bayesian SVAR with informative priors on short-run supply and demand elasticities and an explicit
inventory block. Shocks: oil supply, economic activity, oil consumption demand, oil inventory demand.
Citation: Baumeister, C. and Hamilton, J. D. (2019), "Structural Interpretation of Vector
Autoregressions with Incomplete Identification", American Economic Review 109(5).

##### Kanzig (2021)
Oil supply news shock identified from oil futures price changes in a tight window around OPEC
announcements, used as an external instrument. Positive values mean news of lower future supply.
Citation: Kanzig, D. R. (2021), "The Macroeconomic Effects of Oil Supply News", AER 111(4).

##### Triangulation rule
For each month, the supply-shock sign is taken from each available method (absolute value below
0.25 standard deviations counts as neutral). Agreement labels: "agree" (all non-neutral signs equal),
"partial" (one method neutral), "disagree" (opposite signs). Sign convention is harmonised so that
positive means price-raising (less supply) for every method.

### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/scripts/decompose.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Kilian (2009) SVAR decomposition of the real oil price, triangulated with BH (2019) and Kanzig (2021).

Usage:
  python decompose.py --data kilian_inputs.csv --out shocks.json --brief brief_fa.md
         [--bh bh_shocks.csv --bh-sign more_supply] [--kaenzig kaenzig_monthly.csv]
         [--lags 24] [--bootstrap 300] [--asof YYYY-MM-DD]
"""
import argparse, datetime as dt, json, sys
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

SHOCKS = ["supply", "aggregate_demand", "oil_specific_demand"]
FA_SHOCK = {"supply": "شوک عرضه نفت", "aggregate_demand": "تقاضای کل اقتصاد جهانی",
            "oil_specific_demand": "تقاضای اختصاصی نفت (احتیاطی و موجودی)", "base": "پایه (شرایط اولیه)"}
FA_DATE = str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹")
FA_DIGITS = str.maketrans("0123456789.-", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹٫−")


def fa(x, nd=1, signed=True):
    if x is None:
        return "—"
    return (f"{x:+.{nd}f}" if signed else f"{x:.{nd}f}").translate(FA_DIGITS)


def load_inputs(path):
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    df["month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(pd.to_datetime(df["month"]), freq="M")
    return df.sort_values("month").set_index("month")


def build_y(df):
    dprod = 100 * np.log(df["world_crude_prod_kbd"]).diff()
    rpo = 100 * np.log(df["oil_price_nominal"] / df["us_cpi"])
    return pd.concat({"dprod": dprod, "rea": df["igrea"], "rpo": rpo}, axis=1).dropna()


def var_ols(Y, p):
    T, k = Y.shape
    X = np.hstack([np.ones((T - p, 1))] + [Y[p - i:T - i] for i in range(1, p + 1)])
    Yt = Y[p:]
    B = np.linalg.lstsq(X, Yt, rcond=None)[0]
    U = Yt - X @ B
    Sigma = U.T @ U / (T - p - X.shape[1])
    return B, U, Sigma


def lag_mats(B, k, p):
    return [B[1 + i * k:1 + (i + 1) * k].T for i in range(p)]


def max_root(A, k, p):
    F = np.zeros((k * p, k * p))
    F[:k, :] = np.hstack(A)
    if p > 1:
        F[k:, :-k] = np.eye(k * (p - 1))
    return float(np.max(np.abs(np.linalg.eigvals(F))))


def ma_coefs(A, k, H):
    Phi = [np.eye(k)]
    for h in range(1, H + 1):
        Phi.append(sum(A[i - 1] @ Phi[h - i] for i in range(1, min(h, len(A)) + 1)))
    return Phi


def structural(B, U, Sigma, k, p, H):
    A = lag_mats(B, k, p)
    B0inv = np.linalg.cholesky(Sigma)
    E = U @ np.linalg.inv(B0inv).T
    Theta = [P @ B0inv for P in ma_coefs(A, k, H)]
    return A, B0inv, E, Theta


def irf_table(Theta, H):
    # price response (rpo, index 2) and cumulative production response (dprod, index 0)
    out = {}
    for j, name in enumerate(SHOCKS):
        sign = -1.0 if name == "supply" else 1.0  # supply shown as a disruption (price-raising)
        price = [sign * Theta[h][2, j] for h in range(H + 1)]
        prod = np.cumsum([sign * Theta[h][0, j] for h in range(H + 1)]).tolist()
        rea = [sign * Theta[h][1, j] for h in range(H + 1)]
        out[name] = {"real_price_pct": price, "production_pct_cum": prod, "activity": rea}
    return out


def bootstrap(Y, B, U, k, p, H, draws, seed=11):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    T = Y.shape[0]
    A = lag_mats(B, k, p)
    Uc = U - U.mean(axis=0)
    store = {s: [] for s in SHOCKS}
    for _ in range(draws):
        Ys = np.zeros_like(Y)
        Ys[:p] = Y[:p]
        idx = rng.integers(0, Uc.shape[0], T - p)
        for t in range(p, T):
            Ys[t] = B[0] + sum(A[i] @ Ys[t - 1 - i] for i in range(p)) + Uc[idx[t - p]]
        Bs, Us, Ss = var_ols(Ys, p)
        try:
            _, _, _, Th = structural(Bs, Us, Ss, k, p, H)
        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
            continue
        tab = irf_table(Th, H)
        for s in SHOCKS:
            store[s].append(tab[s]["real_price_pct"])
    bands = {}
    for s in SHOCKS:
        arr = np.array(store[s])
        bands[s] = {q: np.percentile(arr, q, axis=0).round(3).tolist() for q in (5, 16, 84, 95)} if len(arr) else {}
    return bands


def historical_decomposition(rpo, E, Theta):
    n = E.shape[0]
    th = np.array([[Theta[h][2, j] for j in range(3)] for h in range(n)])
    contrib = np.column_stack([np.convolve(E[:, j], th[:, j])[:n] for j in range(3)])
    base = rpo - contrib.sum(axis=1)
    return contrib, base


def window_table(rpo, contrib, base, months):
    rows = {}
    for w in (1, 3, 12):
        if len(rpo) <= w:
            continue
        raw = {name: float(contrib[-1, j] - contrib[-1 - w, j]) for j, name in enumerate(SHOCKS)}
        raw["base"] = float(base[-1] - base[-1 - w])
        total = float(rpo[-1] - rpo[-1 - w])
        d = {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in raw.items()}
        d["total_log_points"] = round(total, 2)
        d["price_change_pct"] = round(100 * (np.exp(total / 100) - 1), 2)
        d["check_sum"] = round(sum(raw.values()) - total, 9)
        d["from"], d["to"] = str(months[-1 - w]), str(months[-1])
        rows[f"{w}m"] = d
    return rows


def load_external(path, col_candidates, flip):
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    df["month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(pd.to_datetime(df["month"]), freq="M")
    col = next((c for c in col_candidates if c in df.columns), None)
    if col is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: none of {col_candidates} found")
    s = df.set_index("month")[col].astype(float)
    s = -s if flip else s
    return s / s.std()


def label(vals, th=0.25):
    signs = [0 if abs(v) < th else (1 if v > 0 else -1) for v in vals if v is not None and not np.isnan(v)]
    nz = [s for s in signs if s != 0]
    if len(signs) < 2:
        return "single_method"
    if not nz:
        return "agree_neutral"
    if all(s == nz[0] for s in nz):
        return "agree" if len(nz) == len(signs) else "partial"
    return "disagree"


FA_LABEL = {"agree": "همسو", "partial": "نیمه‌همسو", "disagree": "ناهمسو", "agree_neutral": "همه خنثی", "single_method": "تک‌روش"}


def write_brief(res, path):
    q = res["quality"]
    if q["status"] == "BLOCK":
        open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("# بریف منتشر نشد\n\n" + "\n".join(f"- {i}" for i in q["issues"]))
        return
    dec, cut = res["decomposition"], res["latest_month"].translate(FA_DATE)
    w = dec.get("12m") or dec.get("3m")
    top = max(SHOCKS, key=lambda s: abs(w[s]))
    move = "افزایش" if w["price_change_pct"] > 0 else "کاهش"
    lines = [f"# چرا قیمت نفت حرکت کرد؟ تجزیه شوک‌ها تا {cut}", "",
             f"> **در ۵ ثانیه:** قیمت واقعی نفت در بازه {w['from'].translate(FA_DATE)} تا {w['to'].translate(FA_DATE)} حدود {fa(abs(w['price_change_pct']), 0, False)}٪ {move} یافت؛ بیشترین سهم تقریبی از «{FA_SHOCK[top]}» بود ({fa(w[top], 0)} واحد لگاریتمی، روش Kilian 2009).", "",
             "## در ۳۰ ثانیه: سهم هر عامل (واحد لگاریتمی ≈ درصد)", "",
             "| بازه | تغییر قیمت واقعی | عرضه | تقاضای اقتصادی | تقاضای اختصاصی نفت | پایه |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    names = {"1m": "۱ ماه", "3m": "۳ ماه", "12m": "۱۲ ماه"}
    for key in ("1m", "3m", "12m"):
        if key in dec:
            d = dec[key]
            lines.append(f"| {names[key]} | {fa(d['price_change_pct'])}٪ | {fa(d['supply'])} | {fa(d['aggregate_demand'])} | {fa(d['oil_specific_demand'])} | {fa(d['base'])} |")
    lines += ["", "## مقایسه روش‌ها: علامت شوک عرضه (مثبت = افزاینده قیمت)", "",
              "| ماه | Kilian | Baumeister–Hamilton | Kanzig | نتیجه |", "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for r in res["triangulation"][-6:]:
        lines.append(f"| {r['month'].translate(FA_DATE)} | {fa(r.get('kilian'))} | {fa(r.get('bh'))} | {fa(r.get('kaenzig'))} | {FA_LABEL[r['label']]} |")
    lines += ["", "## در ۵ دقیقه: جزئیات", ""]
    for s in SHOCKS:
        pr = res["irf"][s]["real_price_pct"]
        h = int(np.argmax(np.abs(pr)))
        lines.append(f"- واکنش قیمت واقعی به یک انحراف معیار «{FA_SHOCK[s]}»: اوج {fa(pr[h])}٪ پس از {fa(h, 0, False)} ماه")
    lines += [f"- مدل: VAR با {fa(res['spec']['lags'], 0, False)} وقفه، نمونه {res['spec']['sample'][0].translate(FA_DATE)} تا {res['spec']['sample'][1].translate(FA_DATE)}، بزرگ‌ترین ریشه {fa(res['spec']['max_root'], 3, False)}",
              "- این اعداد برآورد مدل زیر فرض شناسایی Kilian است (عرضه در همان ماه به تقاضا واکنش نمی‌دهد)؛ مقایسه با Baumeister–Hamilton این فرض را می‌سنجد.",
              "", "## کیفیت داده", "بدون هشدار" if q["status"] == "OK" else "\n".join(f"- ⚠ {i}" for i in q["issues"]),
              "", "---", "منابع: " + "؛ ".join(res["sources"])]
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--data", required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True); ap.add_argument("--brief")
    ap.add_argument("--bh"); ap.add_argument("--bh-sign", choices=["more_supply", "price_raising"], default="more_supply")
    ap.add_argument("--kaenzig"); ap.add_argument("--lags", type=int, default=24)
    ap.add_argument("--bootstrap", type=int, default=0); ap.add_argument("--horizon", type=int, default=18)
    ap.add_argument("--asof")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    asof = pd.Period(a.asof, freq="M") if a.asof else pd.Period(dt.date.today(), freq="M")
    issues, block = [], False
    df = load_inputs(a.data)
    full = pd.period_range(df.index.min(), df.index.max(), freq="M")
    if len(full) != len(df.index):
        block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: {len(full) - len(df.index)} missing months in input")
    if (df[["oil_price_nominal", "us_cpi", "world_crude_prod_kbd"]] <= 0).any().any():
        block = True; issues.append("BLOCK: non-positive price, CPI or production values")
    yd = build_y(df)
    k, p = 3, a.lags
    if len(yd) < 120:
        block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: only {len(yd)} usable observations (need >= 120)")
    result = {"generated_at": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
              "sources": ["Kilian (2009) SVAR estimated in-house on EIA/FRED inputs"], "quality": {}}
    if not block:
        Y = yd.values
        B, U, Sigma = var_ols(Y, p)
        A, B0inv, E, Theta = structural(B, U, Sigma, k, p, max(len(Y), a.horizon))
        root = max_root(A, k, p)
        if root >= 1:
            block = True; issues.append(f"BLOCK: VAR not stable (max root {root:.3f})")
        elif root > 0.98:
            issues.append(f"WARN: VAR close to non-stationary (max root {root:.3f})")
        obs_per_param = (len(Y) - p) / (1 + k * p)
        if obs_per_param < 5:
            issues.append(f"WARN: few observations per equation parameter ({obs_per_param:.1f})")
        months = yd.index[p:]
        rpo = Y[p:, 2]
        contrib, base = historical_decomposition(rpo, E, Theta)
        latest = months[-1]
        if (asof - latest).n > 4:
            issues.append(f"WARN: latest model month {latest} is more than 4 months before {asof}")
        irf = irf_table(Theta, a.horizon)
        if a.bootstrap:
            bands = bootstrap(Y, B, U, k, p, a.horizon, a.bootstrap)
            for s in SHOCKS:
                irf[s]["real_price_bands"] = bands[s]
        es = pd.DataFrame(E, index=months, columns=SHOCKS)
        kil_supply = (-es["supply"]) / es["supply"].std()
        ext = {}
        if a.bh:
            ext["bh"] = load_external(a.bh, ["oil_supply", "supply"], flip=(a.bh_sign == "more_supply"))
            result["sources"].append("Baumeister & Hamilton (2019) structural shocks")
        if a.kaenzig:
            ext["kaenzig"] = load_external(a.kaenzig, ["oil_supply_news", "shock"], flip=False)
            result["sources"].append("Kanzig (2021) oil supply news shocks, CC BY 4.0")
        for name, s in ext.items():
            if latest not in s.index:
                issues.append(f"WARN: {name} file does not cover {latest}")
        tri = []
        for m in months[-24:]:
            row = {"month": str(m), "kilian": round(float(kil_supply[m]), 2)}
            for name, s in ext.items():
                row[name] = round(float(s[m]), 2) if m in s.index else None
            row["label"] = label([row.get("kilian"), row.get("bh"), row.get("kaenzig")])
            tri.append(row)
        result.update({
            "spec": {"variables": ["dprod", "rea", "rpo"], "lags": p, "identification": "recursive (Cholesky), Kilian 2009",
                     "sample": [str(yd.index[0]), str(yd.index[-1])], "max_root": round(root, 4)},
            "latest_month": str(latest),
            "decomposition": window_table(rpo, contrib, base, months),
            "irf": {s: {kk: (np.round(v, 3).tolist() if isinstance(v, list) else v) for kk, v in d.items()} for s, d in irf.items()},
            "structural_shocks_tail": [{"month": str(m), **{s: round(float(es.loc[m, s]), 3) for s in SHOCKS}} for m in months[-24:]],
            "triangulation": tri,
        })
    result["quality"] = {"status": "BLOCK" if block else ("WARN" if issues else "OK"), "issues": issues}
    json.dump(result, open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    if a.brief:
        write_brief(result, a.brief)
    print(f"status={result['quality']['status']} latest={result.get('latest_month')}")
    if block:
        sys.exit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/scripts/fetch_inputs.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fetch Kilian (2009) inputs from EIA API v2 and FRED. Keys from EIA_API_KEY and FRED_API_KEY.

Usage: python fetch_inputs.py --out data/kilian_inputs.csv [--start 1973-01] [--price rac|brent]
IDs marked verify in references/data_sources.md must be confirmed on the first live run.
"""
import argparse, os, sys, time
import pandas as pd
import requests

EIA_INTL = "https://api.eia.gov/v2/international/data/"
EIA_SERIES = "https://api.eia.gov/v2/seriesid/{sid}"
FRED = "https://api.stlouisfed.org/fred/series/observations"


def get(url, params, tries=4):
    delay = 2
    for _ in range(tries):
        try:
            r = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=40)
            if r.status_code == 200:
                return r.json()
            if r.status_code in (429, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                time.sleep(delay); delay *= 2; continue
            sys.exit(f"HTTP {r.status_code} for {url}")
        except requests.RequestException:
            time.sleep(delay); delay *= 2
    sys.exit(f"unreachable: {url}")


def to_month(s):
    return pd.PeriodIndex(pd.to_datetime(s), freq="M")


def world_production(key, start):
    params = {"api_key": key, "frequency": "monthly", "data[0]": "value", "start": start,
              "facets[productId][]": "57", "facets[activityId][]": "1",
              "facets[countryRegionId][]": "WORL", "facets[unit][]": "TBPD", "length": 5000}
    rows = get(EIA_INTL, params).get("response", {}).get("data", [])
    if not rows:
        sys.exit("world production: no rows; verify facet IDs (productId 57, activityId 1, WORL, TBPD)")
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    return pd.Series(df["value"].astype(float).values, index=to_month(df["period"]), name="world_crude_prod_kbd")


def eia_series(key, sid, start, name):
    rows = get(EIA_SERIES.format(sid=sid), {"api_key": key, "start": start, "length": 5000}).get("response", {}).get("data", [])
    if not rows:
        sys.exit(f"{sid}: no rows; verify the series ID")
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    return pd.Series(pd.to_numeric(df["value"], errors="coerce").values, index=to_month(df["period"]), name=name)


def fred(key, sid, start, name):
    obs = get(FRED, {"series_id": sid, "api_key": key, "file_type": "json", "observation_start": start + "-01"})["observations"]
    s = pd.Series(pd.to_numeric([o["value"] for o in obs], errors="coerce"), index=to_month([o["date"] for o in obs]), name=name)
    return s.groupby(level=0).mean()


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True); ap.add_argument("--start", default="1973-01")
    ap.add_argument("--price", choices=["rac", "brent"], default="rac")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    ek, fk = os.environ.get("EIA_API_KEY"), os.environ.get("FRED_API_KEY")
    if not ek or not fk:
        sys.exit("set EIA_API_KEY and FRED_API_KEY")
    prod = world_production(ek, a.start)
    price = eia_series(ek, "PET.R1300____3.M", a.start, "oil_price_nominal") if a.price == "rac" \
        else fred(fk, "MCOILBRENTEU", a.start, "oil_price_nominal")
    df = pd.concat([prod, fred(fk, "IGREA", a.start, "igrea"), price, fred(fk, "CPIAUCSL", a.start, "us_cpi")], axis=1).dropna()
    df.index.name = "month"
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(a.out) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    df.reset_index().assign(month=lambda d: d["month"].astype(str)).to_csv(a.out, index=False)
    print(f"rows={len(df)} from={df.index.min()} to={df.index.max()} price={a.price}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `oil-shock-decomposition/tests/test_synthetic.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline test on SIMULATED data with known structural shocks. Not real oil data.
Run: python tests/test_synthetic.py
"""
import json, os, subprocess, sys, tempfile
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SCRIPT = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "scripts", "decompose.py")


def simulate(T=480, seed=3):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    B0inv = np.array([[1.0, 0, 0], [0.2, 4.0, 0], [-1.5, 2.0, 6.0]])
    A1 = np.array([[0.1, 0, 0], [0.0, 0.85, 0], [0.0, 0.05, 0.9]])
    A2 = np.array([[0.05, 0, 0], [0.0, 0.05, 0], [0.0, 0.0, 0.05]])
    e = rng.standard_normal((T, 3))
    y = np.zeros((T, 3)); y[:, 2] = 400
    for t in range(2, T):
        y[t] = np.array([0, 0, 400 * 0.05]) + A1 @ y[t - 1] + A2 @ y[t - 2] + B0inv @ e[t]
    months = pd.period_range("1985-01", periods=T, freq="M")
    cpi = 100 * np.exp(np.arange(T) * 0.002)
    df = pd.DataFrame({"month": months.astype(str),
                       "world_crude_prod_kbd": 60000 * np.exp(np.cumsum(y[:, 0]) / 100),
                       "igrea": y[:, 1], "oil_price_nominal": np.exp(y[:, 2] / 100) * cpi / 100, "us_cpi": cpi})
    return df, e, months


def main():
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    df, e, months = simulate()
    data = os.path.join(tmp, "in.csv"); df.to_csv(data, index=False)
    kz = pd.DataFrame({"month": months.astype(str), "oil_supply_news": -e[:, 0] + 0.5 * np.random.default_rng(1).standard_normal(len(e))})
    kzp = os.path.join(tmp, "kz.csv"); kz.to_csv(kzp, index=False)
    out, brief = os.path.join(tmp, "s.json"), os.path.join(tmp, "b.md")
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, SCRIPT, "--data", data, "--out", out, "--brief", brief, "--lags", "6",
                    "--bootstrap", "40", "--kaenzig", kzp, "--asof", str(months[-1])], check=True)
    r = json.load(open(out, encoding="utf-8"))
    assert r["quality"]["status"] in ("OK", "WARN"), r["quality"]
    for w, d in r["decomposition"].items():
        assert abs(d["check_sum"]) < 1e-6, (w, d)
    est = pd.DataFrame(r["structural_shocks_tail"]).set_index("month")
    true = pd.DataFrame(e[-24:], index=[str(m) for m in months[-24:]], columns=est.columns)
    corr = {c: float(np.corrcoef(est[c], true[c])[0, 1]) for c in est.columns}
    assert all(v > 0.9 for v in corr.values()), corr
    assert "real_price_bands" in r["irf"]["supply"]
    agree = sum(t["label"] == "agree" for t in r["triangulation"])
    text = open(brief, encoding="utf-8").read()
    assert "Kilian" in text
    bad = df.drop(index=[100]); bp = os.path.join(tmp, "bad.csv"); bad.to_csv(bp, index=False)
    rc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, SCRIPT, "--data", bp, "--out", out]).returncode
    assert rc == 1 and json.load(open(out))["quality"]["status"] == "BLOCK"
    print("shock recovery correlations:", {k: round(v, 3) for k, v in corr.items()}, "agree months:", agree)
    print("ALL TESTS PASSED")
    print(text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ۷-۳. forecast-benchmark

فایل‌ها: `forecast-benchmark/SKILL.md`، `forecast-benchmark/README_fa.md`، `forecast-benchmark/references/interpretation.md`، `forecast-benchmark/references/methods.md`، `forecast-benchmark/scripts/evaluate.py`، `forecast-benchmark/scripts/make_baselines.py`، `forecast-benchmark/tests/test_synthetic.py`

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/SKILL.md`

---
name: forecast-benchmark
description: Hold every oil price forecast to the standard the research literature uses. Builds real-time baselines (no-change, futures-based, rolling AR, drift), evaluates any model's out-of-sample forecasts against the no-change benchmark with MSPE and MAE ratios, the Diebold-Mariano test with the Harvey-Leybourne-Newbold correction and the Pesaran-Timmermann directional test, forms real-time forecast combinations, and produces an empirical fan chart and a Persian scoreboard brief. Use before any forecast is shown on a dashboard, when comparing models, or when asked how reliable a forecast is.
---

#### Forecast Benchmark

The no-change forecast (tomorrow equals today) is notoriously hard to beat for oil prices
(Alquist and Kilian 2010; Baumeister and Kilian 2014, 2015). This skill makes that the default bar:
a model is only promoted to the executive dashboard if it beats no-change out of sample, in real
time, with a test that says the gain is not luck.

##### When to use
- Before publishing or refreshing any price forecast on the platform.
- When a new model (statistical, ML, foundation model, vendor or analyst forecast) is proposed.
- When someone asks "how much can we trust this forecast?" or wants a probability range.

Do NOT use for: explaining past moves (use oil-shock-decomposition) or producing a single model's
forecast from scratch beyond the built-in baselines.

##### Requirements
- Python 3.10+, `numpy`, `pandas`, `scipy`.

##### Inputs
- `actuals.csv`: `date, value` (one price series; monthly or daily; positive values).
- Forecast files (any number), long format: `model, origin, horizon, forecast` and optionally
  `created_at` (when the forecast was actually made; used for the look-ahead check).
  `horizon` is counted in observations of the actuals series (months for monthly data).
- Optional `futures.csv` for the futures baseline: `origin, horizon, price`.

##### Workflow
1. **Baselines** (real time: every forecast uses only data up to its origin):
   `python scripts/make_baselines.py --actuals actuals.csv --horizons 1,3,6,12 --first-origin 2010-01 --out baselines.csv [--futures futures.csv] [--window 120]`
   Models: `no_change`, `drift`, `ar1_logdiff`, `futures` (if given).
2. **Evaluate** your models together with the baselines:
   `python scripts/evaluate.py --actuals actuals.csv --forecasts baselines.csv model_x.csv --out scoreboard.json --brief brief_fa.md [--min-n 30] [--combo-window 36]`
3. **Gate**: if `quality.status` is `BLOCK`, stop and report. Models flagged `lookahead` are excluded.
4. **Promote** only models whose verdict is `significantly_better` for the horizon shown; otherwise
   the dashboard shows the no-change path with the empirical fan chart.
5. **Review** with `references/interpretation.md` and deliver to the destination the job names.

##### Evaluation rules
- Loss: squared and absolute error on price levels (option `--log` for log levels).
- Ratios below 1 mean better than no-change.
- Diebold-Mariano: HAC variance with h-1 Newey-West lags, Harvey-Leybourne-Newbold small-sample
  correction, one-sided p-value (model better than benchmark), t distribution with n-1 df.
- Directional accuracy: share of correct signs of the change from the origin; Pesaran-Timmermann test.
- Combinations (real time): equal weight and inverse-MSPE weights using only errors observed
  before each origin over the last `combo-window` origins.
- Verdicts per model and horizon: `significantly_better` (ratio < 1 and p < 0.05),
  `better_not_significant`, `not_better`, `insufficient_sample` (n < min-n).
- Fan chart: quantiles 5, 16, 50, 84, 95 of past log forecast errors of the promoted model
  (or no-change), applied to its latest live forecast.

##### Quality gates
- `BLOCK`: actuals have duplicates or non-positive values; benchmark forecasts missing for an
  evaluated horizon; no overlap between forecasts and actuals.
- `WARN`: a model has `created_at` later than `origin` (look-ahead, model excluded); fewer than
  `min-n` evaluation points; fewer than 3 origins for a combination weight.

##### Output contract
`scoreboard.json`: `spec`, `scores` (per horizon, per model: n, mspe, mae, mspe_ratio, mae_ratio,
dm_stat, dm_pvalue, hit_rate, pt_pvalue, verdict), `recommendation` (per horizon: model and reason),
`fan_chart` (per horizon: origin, target date, quantiles), `excluded`, `quality`.
`brief_fa.md`: 5-second verdict, 30-second scoreboard, 5-minute detail and fan chart table.

##### Files
- `scripts/make_baselines.py`: real-time baseline forecasts.
- `scripts/evaluate.py`: tests, combinations, verdicts, fan chart, Persian brief.
- `references/methods.md`: formulas and citations.
- `references/interpretation.md`: how to read and present results.
- `tests/test_synthetic.py`: offline test where the futures baseline carries real signal.

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/README_fa.md`

#### راهنمای Skill محک‌زنی پیش‌بینی (forecast-benchmark)

هر پیش‌بینی قیمت نفت پیش از نمایش در داشبورد باید پیش‌بینی «بدون تغییر» را در شرایط واقعی
(بدون نگاه به آینده) و با آزمون معنی‌داری شکست دهد. این Skill مدل‌های پایه را می‌سازد، همه مدل‌ها را
می‌سنجد، ترکیب بهینه می‌سازد، بازه احتمال (نمودار بادبزنی) می‌دهد و بریف فارسی می‌نویسد.

##### اجرا
```
pip install numpy pandas scipy
python tests/test_synthetic.py
python scripts/make_baselines.py --actuals actuals.csv --horizons 1,3,6,12 --first-origin 2010-01 --out baselines.csv
python scripts/evaluate.py --actuals actuals.csv --forecasts baselines.csv my_model.csv --out scoreboard.json --brief brief_fa.md
```

##### نکات
1. ستون created_at در فایل پیش‌بینی نشت اطلاعات آینده را می‌گیرد؛ برای پیش‌بینی‌های فروشندگان و تحلیلگران حتماً پر شود.
2. برای مقایسه منصفانه حداقل ۳۰ پیش‌بینی در هر افق لازم است.
3. اگر هیچ مدلی معنی‌دار بهتر نبود، داشبورد مسیر «بدون تغییر» با بازه احتمال را نشان می‌دهد.

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/references/interpretation.md`

#### Interpretation

- Lead with the verdict, not the model name: "no model beat the no-change forecast" is a valid and
  useful headline for executives.
- A ratio of 0.95 that is not significant is not a win; say "not distinguishable from no-change".
- Directional accuracy above 50 percent can be useful for hedging decisions even when MSPE gains
  are small; report it separately and only when the PT test is significant.
- Results depend on the sample; mention the evaluation period and the number of forecasts.
- Crisis periods (for example the 2026 Hormuz disruption) dominate squared errors; when a single
  period drives the result, say so.
- The fan chart is empirical, not a model of tail risk; add scenario analysis for extreme events.

##### Persian vocabulary
- «پیش‌بینی بدون تغییر» for no-change; «معنی‌دار» for statistically significant;
  «نسبت خطا» for MSPE ratio; «دقت جهت» for hit rate; «بازه احتمال» for fan chart bands.

##### Before you publish
- quality.status is not BLOCK; excluded models are listed.
- Every promoted model has verdict significantly_better for the horizon shown.
- Evaluation period and n are in the brief.
- No number typed by hand.

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/references/methods.md`

#### Methods

##### Loss ratios
MSPE ratio = mean((f_m - y)^2) / mean((f_nc - y)^2); MAE ratio analogous. Computed on the common
set of (origin, horizon) pairs available for both the model and the benchmark.

##### Diebold-Mariano with HLN correction
d_t = L(e_nc,t) - L(e_m,t) with squared loss. DM = mean(d) / sqrt(LRV / n), LRV estimated with
Newey-West using h-1 lags. HLN correction: DM* = DM x sqrt((n + 1 - 2h + h(h-1)/n) / n), compared
with a t distribution with n-1 degrees of freedom. One-sided p-value for H1: model better.
Caveat: for nested models (AR vs no-change) DM is conservative; Clark-West is the formal
alternative and can be added later.
References: Diebold and Mariano (1995); Harvey, Leybourne and Newbold (1997).

##### Pesaran-Timmermann directional test
Compares the hit rate of predicted versus actual signs of (y_target - y_origin) with the rate
expected under independence. References: Pesaran and Timmermann (1992).

##### Real-time combinations
At each origin, weights use only forecast errors whose targets are observed by that origin, over
the last W origins. Inverse-MSPE weights w_m = (1 / MSPE_m) / sum_k (1 / MSPE_k).
Reference: Baumeister and Kilian (2015), "Forecasting the Real Price of Oil in a Changing World:
A Forecast Combination Approach", JBES.

##### Futures-based forecast
Forecast = futures price for the maturity matching the horizon (Alquist and Kilian 2010). It is a
market expectation plus a risk premium; it is not guaranteed to beat no-change.

##### Empirical fan chart
q-quantile of log(y / f) for the model at horizon h over the evaluation sample, applied as
f_live x exp(q). Assumes the error distribution is stable; state that in the brief.

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/scripts/evaluate.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Evaluate oil price forecasts against the no-change benchmark; combinations; fan chart; Persian brief.

Usage: python evaluate.py --actuals actuals.csv --forecasts f1.csv [f2.csv ...] --out scoreboard.json
                          [--brief brief_fa.md] [--min-n 30] [--combo-window 36] [--log]
"""
import argparse, datetime as dt, json, sys
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats

BENCH = "no_change"
FA = str.maketrans("0123456789.-", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹٫−")
FA_DATE = str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹")
V_FA = {"significantly_better": "بهتر، معنی‌دار", "better_not_significant": "بهتر، غیرمعنی‌دار",
        "not_better": "بهتر نیست", "insufficient_sample": "نمونه ناکافی", "benchmark": "معیار"}


def fa(x, nd=2):
    return "—" if x is None or (isinstance(x, float) and np.isnan(x)) else f"{x:.{nd}f}".translate(FA)


def dm_test(e_b, e_m, h):
    d = e_b ** 2 - e_m ** 2
    n = len(d)
    dc = d - d.mean()
    lrv = dc @ dc / n
    for lag in range(1, h):
        lrv += 2 * (1 - lag / h) * (dc[lag:] @ dc[:-lag]) / n
    if lrv <= 0:
        lrv = dc @ dc / n
    dm = d.mean() / np.sqrt(lrv / n) if lrv > 0 else 0.0
    hln = np.sqrt(max((n + 1 - 2 * h + h * (h - 1) / n) / n, 1e-9))
    stat = dm * hln
    return float(stat), float(1 - stats.t.cdf(stat, n - 1))


def pt_test(actual_chg, pred_chg):
    m = (actual_chg != 0) & (pred_chg != 0)
    a, p = actual_chg[m] > 0, pred_chg[m] > 0
    n = int(m.sum())
    if n < 10:
        return None, None
    P = float(np.mean(a == p)); py, px = a.mean(), p.mean()
    ps = py * px + (1 - py) * (1 - px)
    v = ps * (1 - ps) / n
    vs = ((2 * py - 1) ** 2 * px * (1 - px) + (2 * px - 1) ** 2 * py * (1 - py)) / n + 4 * py * px * (1 - py) * (1 - px) / n ** 2
    if v - vs <= 0:
        return P, None
    return P, float(1 - stats.norm.cdf((P - ps) / np.sqrt(v - vs)))


def add_combos(F, E, window, issues):
    cands = [m for m in F["model"].unique() if m != BENCH]
    out = []
    warned = False
    for h, sub in F[F["model"].isin(cands)].groupby("horizon"):
        piv = sub.pivot_table(index="origin", columns="model", values="forecast")
        tdate = sub.groupby("origin")["target_date"].first()
        Eh = E[(E["horizon"] == h) & E["model"].isin(cands)]
        for o, row in piv.iterrows():
            row = row.dropna()
            if row.empty:
                continue
            out.append(("combo_equal", o, h, tdate[o], float(row.mean())))
            past = Eh[Eh["target_date"] <= o]
            recent = past[past["origin"].isin(sorted(past["origin"].unique())[-window:])]
            ms = recent.groupby("model")["sq"].agg(["mean", "count"])
            ms = ms[(ms["count"] >= 3) & ms.index.isin(row.index)]
            if len(ms) >= 1:
                w = (1 / ms["mean"]) / (1 / ms["mean"]).sum()
                out.append(("combo_inv_mspe", o, h, tdate[o], float((row[w.index] * w).sum())))
            else:
                warned = True
    if warned:
        issues.append("WARN: early origins had fewer than 3 observed errors; combo_inv_mspe starts later")
    return pd.DataFrame(out, columns=["model", "origin", "horizon", "target_date", "forecast"])


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--actuals", required=True); ap.add_argument("--forecasts", nargs="+", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True); ap.add_argument("--brief")
    ap.add_argument("--min-n", type=int, default=30); ap.add_argument("--combo-window", type=int, default=36)
    ap.add_argument("--log", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    issues, block, excluded = [], False, []
    act = pd.read_csv(a.actuals, parse_dates=["date"]).sort_values("date")
    if act["date"].duplicated().any() or (act["value"] <= 0).any():
        block = True; issues.append("BLOCK: actuals have duplicate dates or non-positive values")
    if block:
        res = {"generated_at": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"), "excluded": [],
               "quality": {"status": "BLOCK", "issues": issues}}
        json.dump(res, open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        if a.brief:
            write_brief(res, a.brief)
        print("status=BLOCK")
        sys.exit(1)
    yv = act.set_index("date")["value"].astype(float)
    F = pd.concat([pd.read_csv(f) for f in a.forecasts], ignore_index=True)
    for c in ("origin", "target_date"):
        F[c] = pd.to_datetime(F[c])
    if "created_at" in F.columns:
        F["created_at"] = pd.to_datetime(F["created_at"])
        bad = sorted(F.loc[F["created_at"] > F["origin"], "model"].unique())
        for m in bad:
            excluded.append({"model": m, "reason": "lookahead: created_at later than origin"})
            issues.append(f"WARN: model {m} excluded (look-ahead)")
        F = F[~F["model"].isin(bad)]
    F = F.drop_duplicates(["model", "origin", "horizon"], keep="last")[["model", "origin", "horizon", "target_date", "forecast"]]
    nc = F[["origin", "horizon", "target_date"]].drop_duplicates()
    nc = nc[nc["origin"].isin(yv.index)].assign(model=BENCH, forecast=lambda d: yv.reindex(d["origin"]).to_numpy())
    F = pd.concat([F[F["model"] != BENCH], nc], ignore_index=True)
    F["y"] = yv.reindex(F["target_date"]).to_numpy(); F["y0"] = yv.reindex(F["origin"]).to_numpy()
    tf = (lambda x: np.log(x)) if a.log else (lambda x: x)
    E = F.dropna(subset=["y"]).assign(sq=lambda d: (tf(d["forecast"]) - tf(d["y"])) ** 2)
    if E.empty:
        block = True; issues.append("BLOCK: no overlap between forecasts and actuals")
    result = {"generated_at": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"), "excluded": excluded}
    if not block:
        C = add_combos(F[["model", "origin", "horizon", "target_date", "forecast"]], E, a.combo_window, issues)
        C["y"] = yv.reindex(C["target_date"]).to_numpy(); C["y0"] = yv.reindex(C["origin"]).to_numpy()
        F = pd.concat([F, C], ignore_index=True)
        ev = F.dropna(subset=["y", "y0"])
        scores, rec, fan = {}, {}, {}
        for h, sub in ev.groupby("horizon"):
            b = sub[sub["model"] == BENCH].set_index("origin")
            hs = {}
            for m, sm in sub.groupby("model"):
                sm = sm.set_index("origin")
                idx = sm.index.intersection(b.index)
                n = len(idx)
                fm, fb, y, y0 = (tf(sm.loc[idx, "forecast"]).to_numpy(), tf(b.loc[idx, "forecast"]).to_numpy(),
                                 tf(sm.loc[idx, "y"]).to_numpy(), tf(sm.loc[idx, "y0"]).to_numpy())
                em, eb = fm - y, fb - y
                r = {"n": n, "mspe": float(np.mean(em ** 2)), "mae": float(np.mean(np.abs(em)))}
                r["mspe_ratio"] = r["mspe"] / float(np.mean(eb ** 2)) if n else None
                r["mae_ratio"] = r["mae"] / float(np.mean(np.abs(eb))) if n else None
                if m == BENCH:
                    r.update(dm_stat=None, dm_pvalue=None, hit_rate=None, pt_pvalue=None, verdict="benchmark")
                else:
                    r["dm_stat"], r["dm_pvalue"] = dm_test(eb, em, int(h)) if n > 2 else (None, None)
                    r["hit_rate"], r["pt_pvalue"] = pt_test(y - y0, fm - y0)
                    if n < a.min_n:
                        r["verdict"] = "insufficient_sample"
                    elif r["mspe_ratio"] < 1 and r["dm_pvalue"] is not None and r["dm_pvalue"] < 0.05:
                        r["verdict"] = "significantly_better"
                    elif r["mspe_ratio"] < 1:
                        r["verdict"] = "better_not_significant"
                    else:
                        r["verdict"] = "not_better"
                hs[m] = {k: (round(v, 4) if isinstance(v, float) else v) for k, v in r.items()}
            if all(v["n"] < a.min_n for v in hs.values()):
                issues.append(f"WARN: horizon {h} has fewer than {a.min_n} evaluation points")
            scores[str(h)] = dict(sorted(hs.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]["mspe_ratio"] or 9))
            winners = [m for m, v in hs.items() if v["verdict"] == "significantly_better"]
            best = min(winners, key=lambda m: hs[m]["mspe_ratio"]) if winners else BENCH
            rec[str(h)] = {"model": best, "reason": "lowest MSPE ratio among significant models" if winners else "no model beats no-change significantly"}
            err = np.log(sub.loc[sub["model"] == best, "y"] / sub.loc[sub["model"] == best, "forecast"])
            live = F[(F["model"] == best) & (F["horizon"] == h) & F["y"].isna()].sort_values("origin")
            if len(err) >= 10 and not live.empty:
                lv = live.iloc[-1]
                fan[str(h)] = {"model": best, "origin": lv["origin"].date().isoformat(), "target_date": lv["target_date"].date().isoformat(),
                               "point": round(float(lv["forecast"]), 3),
                               "quantiles": {str(q): round(float(lv["forecast"] * np.exp(np.quantile(err, q / 100))), 3) for q in (5, 16, 50, 84, 95)}}
        result.update({"spec": {"benchmark": BENCH, "loss": "log levels" if a.log else "levels", "min_n": a.min_n,
                                "evaluation_period": [ev["origin"].min().date().isoformat(), ev["origin"].max().date().isoformat()],
                                "models": sorted(F["model"].unique().tolist())},
                       "scores": scores, "recommendation": rec, "fan_chart": fan})
    result["quality"] = {"status": "BLOCK" if block else ("WARN" if issues else "OK"), "issues": issues}
    json.dump(result, open(a.out, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str)
    if a.brief:
        write_brief(result, a.brief)
    print(f"status={result['quality']['status']} horizons={list(result.get('scores', {}).keys())}")
    sys.exit(1 if block else 0)


def write_brief(r, path):
    q = r["quality"]
    if q["status"] == "BLOCK":
        open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("# بریف منتشر نشد\n\n" + "\n".join(f"- {i}" for i in q["issues"]))
        return
    rec = r["recommendation"]
    wins = {h: v["model"] for h, v in rec.items() if v["model"] != BENCH}
    hz = "، ".join(h.translate(FA_DATE) for h in wins) if wins else ""
    nohz = "، ".join(h.translate(FA_DATE) for h in rec if h not in wins)
    if wins and not nohz:
        head = f"در همه افق‌های بررسی‌شده ({hz} دوره) حداقل یک مدل به‌طور معنی‌دار از پیش‌بینی «بدون تغییر» بهتر بود."
    elif wins:
        head = f"در افق(های) {hz} دوره یک مدل به‌طور معنی‌دار از «بدون تغییر» بهتر بود؛ در افق(های) {nohz} داشبورد مسیر «بدون تغییر» را نشان می‌دهد."
    else:
        head = "هیچ مدلی به‌طور معنی‌دار از پیش‌بینی «بدون تغییر» بهتر نبود؛ داشبورد مسیر «بدون تغییر» را با بازه احتمال نشان می‌دهد."
    p0, p1 = (x.translate(FA_DATE) for x in r["spec"]["evaluation_period"])
    L = ["# محک‌زنی پیش‌بینی‌های قیمت نفت", "", f"> **در ۵ ثانیه:** {head}", "",
         f"دوره ارزیابی: {p0} تا {p1} · معیار: پیش‌بینی «بدون تغییر» · نسبت خطای کمتر از ۱ یعنی بهتر", "",
         "## در ۳۰ ثانیه: جدول امتیاز"]
    for h, models in r["scores"].items():
        L += ["", f"### افق {h.translate(FA_DATE)} دوره · پیشنهاد: `{rec[h]['model']}`", "",
              "| مدل | تعداد | نسبت خطا (MSPE) | مقدار p (DM) | دقت جهت | حکم |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
        for m, v in models.items():
            hr = fa(100 * v["hit_rate"], 0) + "٪" if v.get("hit_rate") is not None else "—"
            L.append(f"| `{m}` | {str(v['n']).translate(FA_DATE)} | {fa(v['mspe_ratio'])} | {fa(v.get('dm_pvalue'), 3)} | {hr} | {V_FA[v['verdict']]} |")
    if r["fan_chart"]:
        L += ["", "## در ۵ دقیقه: بازه احتمال آخرین پیش‌بینی (نمودار بادبزنی تجربی)", "",
              "| افق | تاریخ هدف | ۵٪ | ۱۶٪ | میانه | ۸۴٪ | ۹۵٪ | مدل |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
        for h, f in r["fan_chart"].items():
            qq = f["quantiles"]
            L.append(f"| {h.translate(FA_DATE)} | {f['target_date'].translate(FA_DATE)} | {fa(qq['5'])} | {fa(qq['16'])} | {fa(qq['50'])} | {fa(qq['84'])} | {fa(qq['95'])} | `{f['model']}` |")
        L.append("")
        L.append("- بازه‌ها از توزیع خطاهای گذشته همان مدل ساخته شده‌اند و ریسک رویدادهای کم‌سابقه را کامل نشان نمی‌دهند؛ برای آن از تحلیل سناریو استفاده شود.")
    if r["excluded"]:
        L += ["", "مدل‌های کنارگذاشته‌شده: " + "، ".join(f"`{e['model']}`" for e in r["excluded"])]
    L += ["", "## کیفیت ارزیابی", "بدون هشدار" if q["status"] == "OK" else "\n".join(f"- ⚠ {i}" for i in q["issues"]),
          "", "---", "روش: Diebold–Mariano با اصلاح Harvey–Leybourne–Newbold، آزمون جهت Pesaran–Timmermann، ترکیب بلادرنگ Baumeister–Kilian."]
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(L) + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/scripts/make_baselines.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Real-time baseline forecasts: no_change, drift, ar1_logdiff, futures (optional).

Usage: python make_baselines.py --actuals actuals.csv --horizons 1,3,6,12 --first-origin 2010-01
                                --out baselines.csv [--futures futures.csv] [--window 120]
Every forecast made at origin t uses only observations up to and including t.
"""
import argparse
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


def offset(dates):
    step = float(np.median(np.diff(dates.values).astype("timedelta64[D]").astype(float)))
    return (lambda h: pd.DateOffset(months=h)) if step >= 27 else (lambda h: pd.offsets.BDay(h))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--actuals", required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--horizons", default="1,3,6,12"); ap.add_argument("--first-origin", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--futures"); ap.add_argument("--window", type=int, default=120)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    act = pd.read_csv(a.actuals, parse_dates=["date"]).sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    y, dates = act["value"].to_numpy(float), act["date"]
    H = [int(h) for h in a.horizons.split(",")]
    step = offset(dates)
    dl = np.diff(np.log(y))  # dl[j] = log change from j to j+1
    fut = {}
    if a.futures:
        f = pd.read_csv(a.futures, parse_dates=["origin"])
        fut = {(o, int(h)): float(p) for o, h, p in zip(f["origin"], f["horizon"], f["price"])}
    first = int(np.argmax(dates >= pd.Timestamp(a.first_origin)))
    rows = []
    for i in range(first, len(y)):
        o = dates[i]
        win = dl[max(0, i - a.window):i]
        mu = float(win.mean()) if len(win) else 0.0
        c = phi = 0.0
        if len(win) > 12:
            X = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(win) - 1), win[:-1]])
            c, phi = np.linalg.lstsq(X, win[1:], rcond=None)[0]
        for h in H:
            t = i + h
            tdate = dates[t] if t < len(y) else o + step(h)
            g, s = (win[-1] if len(win) else 0.0), 0.0
            for _ in range(h):
                g = c + phi * g; s += g
            base = {"no_change": y[i], "drift": y[i] * np.exp(mu * h), "ar1_logdiff": y[i] * np.exp(s)}
            if (o, h) in fut:
                base["futures"] = fut[(o, h)]
            for m, v in base.items():
                rows.append((m, o.date().isoformat(), h, pd.Timestamp(tdate).date().isoformat(), round(float(v), 6), o.date().isoformat()))
    pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["model", "origin", "horizon", "target_date", "forecast", "created_at"]).to_csv(a.out, index=False)
    print(f"rows={len(rows)} origins={len(y) - first} horizons={H}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### فایل `forecast-benchmark/tests/test_synthetic.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline test on SIMULATED prices where the futures curve carries real signal. Not real oil data.
Run: python tests/test_synthetic.py
"""
import json, os, subprocess, sys, tempfile
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
S = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "scripts")


def simulate(T=360, phi=0.8, seed=5):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    s = np.zeros(T); x = np.zeros(T); x[0] = np.log(70)
    for t in range(1, T):
        s[t] = phi * s[t - 1] + rng.normal(0, 0.03)
        x[t] = x[t - 1] + s[t - 1] + rng.normal(0, 0.04)
    dates = pd.date_range("1996-01-01", periods=T, freq="MS")
    act = pd.DataFrame({"date": dates.date, "value": np.exp(x)})
    fut = [(dates[i].date(), h, float(np.exp(x[i] + s[i] * (1 - phi ** h) / (1 - phi)))) for i in range(T) for h in (1, 3, 6, 12)]
    return act, pd.DataFrame(fut, columns=["origin", "horizon", "price"])


def main():
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    act, fut = simulate()
    ap, fp = os.path.join(tmp, "a.csv"), os.path.join(tmp, "f.csv")
    act.to_csv(ap, index=False); fut.to_csv(fp, index=False)
    bp = os.path.join(tmp, "b.csv")
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(S, "make_baselines.py"), "--actuals", ap, "--horizons", "1,3,6,12",
                    "--first-origin", "2006-01-01", "--out", bp, "--futures", fp], check=True)
    b = pd.read_csv(bp)
    cheat = b[b["model"] == "no_change"].copy()
    cheat["model"] = "cheater"; cheat["created_at"] = pd.to_datetime(cheat["target_date"]).dt.date
    cp = os.path.join(tmp, "c.csv"); cheat.to_csv(cp, index=False)
    out, brief = os.path.join(tmp, "s.json"), os.path.join(tmp, "br.md")
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(S, "evaluate.py"), "--actuals", ap, "--forecasts", bp, cp,
                    "--out", out, "--brief", brief], check=True)
    r = json.load(open(out, encoding="utf-8"))
    assert r["quality"]["status"] in ("OK", "WARN"), r["quality"]
    assert any(e["model"] == "cheater" for e in r["excluded"])
    assert r["scores"]["1"]["futures"]["verdict"] == "significantly_better", r["scores"]["1"]["futures"]
    assert r["scores"]["1"]["no_change"]["verdict"] == "benchmark"
    assert "combo_equal" in r["scores"]["3"]
    assert "1" in r["fan_chart"] and r["fan_chart"]["1"]["quantiles"]["5"] < r["fan_chart"]["1"]["quantiles"]["95"]
    dup = pd.concat([act, act.iloc[[5]]]); dp = os.path.join(tmp, "d.csv"); dup.to_csv(dp, index=False)
    rc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(S, "evaluate.py"), "--actuals", dp, "--forecasts", bp, "--out", out]).returncode
    assert rc == 1 and json.load(open(out, encoding="utf-8"))["quality"]["status"] == "BLOCK"
    print({h: {m: (v["mspe_ratio"], v["verdict"]) for m, v in ms.items()} for h, ms in r["scores"].items()})
    print("ALL TESTS PASSED")
    print(open(brief, encoding="utf-8").read())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# بخش ۸. کاکپیت مدیریتی نمونه

نسخه متنی کاکپیت؛ نمودارها حذف شده‌اند. اعداد از اجرای سه Skill روی داده شبیه‌سازی‌شده آمده‌اند و وضعیت واقعی بازار را نشان نمی‌دهند.

اتاق وضعیت نفت
خلاصه مدیریتی
موجودی هفتگی آمریکا

## کاکپیت مدیریتی بازار نفت

نمونه طراحی · داده شبیه‌سازی‌شده

خروجی سه Skill · اجرا ۳۰ اکتبر ۲۰۲۶

### خلاصه مدیریتی

برنت
WTI
دوبی

این صفحه نمونه طراحی است. همه اعداد خروجی اجرای واقعی سه Skill (eia-weekly-stocks، oil-shock-decomposition، forecast-benchmark) روی داده شبیه‌سازی‌شده‌اند و وضعیت واقعی بازار را نشان نمی‌دهند. با اتصال کلیدهای EIA و FRED، همین صفحه با داده واقعی پر می‌شود.

موجودی تجاری نفت خام آمریکا
۴۱۵٫۰ mb
۰٫۸ کاهش نسبی به الگوی ۵ ساله

موجودی کاشینگ
۲۲٫۳ mb
۰٫۲+ هفتگی · همانند الگوی فصلی

قیمت واقعی نفت · ۱۲ ماه
۱۲٫۹+٪
تا ۲۰۲۶-۰۸ · محرک اصلی: تقاضای اختصاصی

پیش‌بینی ۱ ماهه (مدل تأییدشده)
۷۴٫۶ $
۳٫۶٪+ نسبت به آخرین قیمت (۷۲٫۰)

#### سه پاسخ در ۵ ثانیه

هر خط به‌طور خودکار از بریف همان Skill آمده است

الان بازار کجاست؟موجودی بنزین آمریکا ۴٫۱ میلیون بشکه افزایش یافت در حالی که الگوی فصلی ۵ ساله ۰٫۱ کاهش است؛ ارزیابی: فشار نزولی. نفت خام و کاشینگ خنثی.فشار نزولی
چرا حرکت کرد؟از ۱۲٫۹٪ افزایش قیمت واقعی در ۱۲ ماه تا اوت ۲۰۲۶، سهم تقریبی تقاضای اختصاصی نفت ۱۷+ واحد و عرضه ۶+ بود؛ تقاضای اقتصادی ۱۱ واحد از آن کاست (روش Kilian 2009).ریسک احتیاطی
به کجا می‌رود؟مدل مبتنی بر قیمت آتی در هر چهار افق به‌طور معنی‌دار از «بدون تغییر» بهتر بود؛ میانه ۳ ماهه ۷۸٫۲ دلار با بازه ۶۸٪ احتمال ۷۰٫۸ تا ۸۵٫۳.مدل تأییدشده

### موجودی هفتگی آمریکا · هفته منتهی به ۲۰۲۶-۱۰-۲۳

Skill: eia-weekly-stocks · منبع طراحی‌شده: EIA WPSR

#### موجودی تجاری نفت خام در برابر میانگین ۵ ساله همان هفته (میلیون بشکه)

۲۰۲۶میانگین ۵ ساله

موجودی در ۲۶ هفته از ۴۴۲٫۰ به ۴۱۵٫۰ رسید و اکنون ۱٫۷ میلیون بشکه زیر میانگین ۵ ساله است.

#### سیگنال‌های این هفته

نسبت به الگوی فصلی

بنزین · ۴٫۲+ میلیون بشکهفشار نزولی
تقطیری · ۰٫۸+خنثی
نفت خام · ۰٫۸−خنثی
کاشینگ · ۰٫۰خنثی
تقاضای ضمنی ۴ هفته · ۰٫۳−٪ سالانهخنثی

آستانه سیگنال: ۱٫۵ میلیون بشکه برای نفت خام و ۱٫۰ برای فرآورده‌ها فاصله از تغییر معمول همین هفته.

### چرا قیمت حرکت کرد؟ · داده تا ۲۰۲۶-۰۸

Skill: oil-shock-decomposition · Kilian (2009) + Baumeister–Hamilton + Kanzig

#### سهم هر عامل در تغییر ماهانه قیمت واقعی (واحد لگاریتمی)

تقاضای اختصاصیتقاضای اقتصادیعرضه

تقاضای اختصاصی نفت (احتیاطی و موجودی) بزرگ‌ترین محرک ماه‌به‌ماه است؛ نشانگر کوچک کنار هر ستون تغییر کل است.

#### سهم عوامل در بازه‌ها

واحد لگاریتمی ≈ ٪

| عامل | ۱ ماه | ۳ ماه | ۱۲ ماه |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| تقاضای اختصاصی | ۶٫۴+ | ۲۴٫۲+ | ۱۷٫۲+ |
| عرضه | ۰٫۷− | ۲٫۶+ | ۶٫۲+ |
| تقاضای اقتصادی | ۱٫۶− | ۰٫۰ | ۱۱٫۲− |
| پایه | ۰٫۰ | ۰٫۰ | ۰٫۰ |
| **جمع (لگاریتمی)** | ۴٫۱+ | ۲۶٫۸+ | ۱۲٫۱+ |
| تغییر قیمت واقعی | ۴٫۲+٪ | ۳۰٫۷+٪ | ۱۲٫۹+٪ |

#### مقایسه روش‌ها: علامت شوک عرضه

مثبت = افزاینده قیمت · انحراف معیار

| ماه | Kilian | Baumeister–Hamilton | Kanzig | نتیجه |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ۲۰۲۶-۰۳ | ۰٫۳− | ۰٫۳+ | ۰٫۶− | ناهمسو |
| ۲۰۲۶-۰۴ | ۰٫۶+ | ۱٫۴+ | ۰٫۵+ | همسو |
| ۲۰۲۶-۰۵ | ۱٫۴+ | ۱٫۵+ | ۱٫۵+ | همسو |
| ۲۰۲۶-۰۶ | ۰٫۰ | ۰٫۷− | ۰٫۰ | نیمه‌همسو |
| ۲۰۲۶-۰۷ | ۰٫۴+ | ۱٫۱+ | ۰٫۴− | ناهمسو |
| ۲۰۲۶-۰۸ | ۰٫۳− | ۱٫۲− | ۱٫۲− | همسو |

در ماه‌های «ناهمسو» نتیجه به این فرض وابسته است که تولیدکنندگان چقدر سریع واکنش نشان می‌دهند؛ داشبورد برنده انتخاب نمی‌کند.

#### اوج واکنش قیمت به یک شوک

یک انحراف معیار

### پیش‌بینی و بازه احتمال · آخرین قیمت ۲۰۲۶-۰۹

Skill: forecast-benchmark · ارزیابی ۲۰۰۸-۰۱ تا ۲۰۲۶-۰۸

#### مسیر قیمت و نمودار بادبزنی پیش‌بینی (دلار بر بشکه)

واقعیمیانه و بازه‌های ۶۸٪ و ۹۰٪

بازه احتمال با افق باز می‌شود: در ۱۲ ماه بازه ۹۰٪ از ۴۷ تا ۱۶۴ دلار است و نباید به‌عنوان عدد قطعی ارائه شود.

#### بازه‌های احتمال

مدل: futures

| افق | میانه | ۶۸٪ | ۹۰٪ |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ۱ ماه · ۲۰۲۶-۱۰ | ۷۴٫۶ | ۷۱٫۳–۷۷٫۸ | ۶۹٫۸–۷۹٫۹ |
| ۳ ماه · ۲۰۲۶-۱۲ | ۷۸٫۲ | ۷۰٫۸–۸۵٫۳ | ۶۷٫۱–۹۱٫۷ |
| ۶ ماه · ۲۰۲۷-۰۳ | ۸۲٫۴ | ۶۷٫۵–۹۹٫۱ | ۵۸٫۲–۱۰۸٫۳ |
| ۱۲ ماه · ۲۰۲۷-۰۹ | ۸۳٫۴ | ۵۵٫۳–۱۲۹٫۲ | ۴۶٫۸–۱۶۳٫۶ |

بازه‌ها از خطاهای گذشته همین مدل ساخته شده‌اند؛ ریسک رویدادهای کم‌سابقه مثل بسته شدن تنگه‌ها باید با تحلیل سناریو سنجیده شود.

### کیفیت داده و روش

#### eia-weekly-stocks

OK
۱۵ سری دریافت شد، همه در هفته ۲۰۲۶-۱۰-۲۳؛ بدون جهش غیرعادی و بدون اصلاحیه.

#### oil-shock-decomposition

OK
VAR پایدار (بزرگ‌ترین ریشه ۰٫۹۷۳)، جمع سهم‌ها با تغییر واقعی برابر؛ داده ماهانه ۲ ماه تأخیر دارد.

#### forecast-benchmark

WARN
ترکیب وزنی از ابتدای دوره خطای کافی نداشت و دیرتر شروع شد؛ هیچ مدلی از اطلاعات آینده استفاده نکرد.

روش‌ها: مقایسه با الگوی فصلی ۵ ساله همان هفته (EIA WPSR)؛ تجزیه شوک Kilian (2009) با مقایسه Baumeister–Hamilton (2019) و Kanzig (2021)؛ آزمون Diebold–Mariano با اصلاح HLN و آزمون جهت Pesaran–Timmermann. همه اعداد این نمونه از اجرای این سه Skill روی داده شبیه‌سازی‌شده آمده‌اند و نباید برای تصمیم‌گیری استفاده شوند.
